@classmethod\\n172:     @functools.cache\\n173:     def _get_cython_function(\\n174:         cls, kind: str, how: str, dtype: np.dtype, is_numeric: bool\\n175:     ):\\n176:         dtype_str = str) -> str:\n   165→        if how in cls._CYTHON_FUNCTIONS[\"aggregate\"]:\n   166→            return \"aggregate\"\n   167→        return \"transform\"\n   168→\n   169→    # Note: we make this a classmethod and pass kind+how so that caching\n   170→    #  works at the class level and not the instance level\n   171→    @classmethod\n   172→    @functools.cache\n   173→    def _get_cython_function(\n   174→        cls, kind: str, how: str, dtype: np.dtype, is_numeric: bool\n   175→    ):\n   176→        dtype_str = dtype.name\n   177→        ftype = cls._CYTHON_FUNCTIONS[kind][how]\n   178→\n   179→        # see if there is a fused-type version of function\n   180→        # only valid for numeric\n   181→        if callable(ftype):\n   182→            f = ftype\n   183→        else:\n   184→            f = getattr(libgroupby, ftype)\n   185→        if is_numeric:\n   186→            return f\n   187→        elif dtype == np.dtype(object):\n   188→            if how in [\"median\", \"cumprod\"]:\n   189→                # no fused types -> no __signatures__\n   190→                raise NotImplementedError(\n   191→                    f\"function is not implemented for this dtype: \"\n   192→                    f\"[how->{how},dtype->{dtype_str}]\"\n   193→                )\n   194→            elif how in [\"std\", \"sem\", \"idxmin\", \"idxmax\"]:\n   195→                # We have a partial object that does not have __signatures__\n   196→                return f\n   197→            elif how in [\"skew\", \"kurt\"]:\n   198→                # _get_cython_vals will convert to float64\n   199→                pass\n   200→            elif \"object\" not in f.__signatures__:\n   201→                # raise NotImplementedError here rather than TypeError later\n   202→                raise NotImplementedError(\n   203→                    f\"function is not implemented for this dtype: \"\n   204→                    f\"[how->{how},dtype->{dtype_str}]\"\n   205→                )\n   206→            return f\n   207→        else:\n   208→            raise NotImplementedError(\n   209→                \"This should not be reached. Please report a bug at \"\n   210→                \"github.com/pandas-dev/pandas/\",\n   211→                dtype,\n   212→            )\n   213→\n   214→    def _get_cython_vals(self, values: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n   215→        \"\"\"\n   216→        Cast numeric dtypes to float64 for functions that only support that.\n   217→\n   218→        Parameters\n   219→        ----------\n   220→        values : np.ndarray\n   221→\n   222→        Returns\n   223→        -------\n   224→        values : np.ndarray\n   225→        \"\"\"\n   226→        how = self.how\n   227→\n   228→        if how in [\"median\", \"std\", \"sem\", \"skew\", \"kurt\"]:\n   229→            # median only has a float64 implementation\n   230→            # We should only get here with is_numeric, as non-numeric cases\n   231→            #  should raise in _get_cython_function\n   232→            values = ensure_float64(values)\n   233→\n   234→        elif values.dtype.kind in \"iu\":\n   235→ _get_output_shape(self, ngroups: int, values: np.ndarray) -> Shape:\n   252→        how = self.how\n   253→        kind = self.kind\n   254→\n   255→        arity = self._cython_arity.get(how, 1)\n   256→\n   257→        out_shape: Shape\n   258→        if how == \"ohlc\":\n   259→            out_shape = (ngroups, arity)\n   260→        elif arity > 1:\n   261→            raise NotImplementedError(\n   262→                \"arity of more than 1 is not supported for the 'how' argument\"\n   263→            )\n   264→        elif kind == \"transform\":\n   265→ 276→            # The Cython implementation only produces the row number; we'll take\n   277→            # from the index using this in post processing\n   278→            out_dtype = \"intp\"\n np.dtype(out_dtype)\n   284→\n   285→    def _get_result_dtype(self, dtype: np.dtype) -> np.dtype:\n   286→        \"\"\"\n   287→        Get the desired dtype of a result based on the\n   288→        input dtype and how it was computed.\n   289→\n   290→        Parameters\n   291→        ----------\n   292→        dtype : np.dtype\n   293→\n   294→        Returns\n   295→        -------\n   296→        np.dtype\n   297→            The desired dtype of the result.\n   298→        \"\"\"\n   299→        how = self.how\n   300→\n   301→        if how in [\"sum\", \"cumsum\", \"sum\", \"prod\", \"cumprod\"]:\n   302→            if dtype == np.dtype(bool):\n   303→                return np.dtype(np.int64)\n   304→        elif how in [\"mean\", \"median\", \"var\", \"std\", \"sem\"]:\n   305→ dtype\n   310→\n   311→    @final\n   312→    def _cython_op_ndim_compat(\n   313→        self,\n   314→        values: np.ndarray,\n   315→        *,\n   316→        min_count: int,\n   317→        ngroups: int,\n   318→        comp_ids: np.ndarray,\n   319→        mask: npt.NDArray[np.bool_] | None = None,\n   320→        result_mask: npt.NDArray[np.bool_] | None = None,\n   321→            )\n   341→            if res.shape[0] == 1:\n   342→                return res[0]\n   343→\n   344→            # otherwise we have OHLC\n   345→            return res.T\n   346→\n   347→ _call_cython_op(\n   360→        self,\n   361→        values: np.ndarray,  # np.ndarray[ndim=2]\n   362→        *,\n   363→        min_count: int,\n   364→        ngroups: int,\n   365→        comp_ids: np.ndarray,\n   366→        mask: npt.NDArray[np.bool_] | None,\n   367→        result_mask: npt.NDArray[np.bool_] | None,\n   368→        initial: Any = 0,\n   369→        **kwargs,\n   370→    ) -> np.ndarray:  # np.ndarray[ndim=2]\n   371→        orig_values = values\n   372→\n   373→        dtype = values.dtype\n   374→        is_numeric = dtype.kind in \"iufcb\"\n   375→\n   376→        is_datetimelike = dtype.kind in \"mM\"\n   377→\n   378→        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n   379→            if mask is None:\n   380→                mask = isna(values)\n   381→\n   382→        if is_datetimelike:\n   383→            values = values.view(\"int64\")\n   384→            is_numeric = True\n   385→        elif dtype.kind == \"b\":\n   386→            values = values.view(\"uint8\")\n   387→        if values.dtype == \"float16\":\n   388→            values = values.astype(np.float32)\n   389→\n   390→        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n   391→            if dtype == object:\n   392→                if kwargs[\"skipna\"]:\n   393→                    # GH#37501: don't raise on pd.NA when skipna=True\n   394→                    if mask is not None and mask.any():\n   395→                        # mask on original values computed separately\n   396→                        values = values.copy()\n   397→                        values[mask] = True\n   398→            values = values.astype(bool, copy=False).view(np.int8)\n   399→            is_numeric = True\n   400→\n   401→        values = values.T\n   402→        if mask is not None:\n   403→            mask = mask.T\n   404→            if result_mask is not None:\n   405→                result_mask = result_mask.T\n   406→\n   407→        out_shape = self._get_output_shape(ngroups, values)\n   408→        func = self._get_cython_function(self.kind, self.how, values.dtype, is_numeric)\n   409→        values = self._get_cython_vals(values)\n   410→        out_dtype = self._get_out_dtype(values.dtype)\n   411→\n   412→        result = maybe_fill(np.empty(out_shape, dtype=out_dtype))\n   413→        if self.kind == \"aggregate\":\n   414→            counts = np.zeros(ngroups, dtype=np.int64)\n   415→            if self.how in [\n   416→                \"idxmin\",\n   417→                \"idxmax\",\n   418→                \"min\",\n   419→                \"max\",\n   420→                \"mean\",\n   421→                \"last\",\n   422→                \"first\",\n   423→                \"sum\",\n   424→                \"median\",\n   425→            ]:\n   426→                if self.how == \"sum\":\n   427→                    # pass in through kwargs only for sum (other functions don't have\n   428→                    # the keyword)\n   429→                    kwargs[\"initial\"] = initial\n   430→                func(\n   431→                    out=result,\n   432→                    counts=counts,\n   433→                    values=values,\n   434→                    labels=comp_ids,\n   435→                    min_count=min_count,\n   436→                    mask=mask,\n   437→                    result_mask=result_mask,\n   438→                    is_datetimelike=is_datetimelike,\n   439→                    **kwargs,\n   440→                )\n   441→            elif self.how in [\"sem\", \"std\", \"var\", \"ohlc\", \"prod\"]:\n   442→                if self.how in [\"std\", \"sem\"]:\n   443→                    kwargs[\"is_datetimelike\"] = is_datetimelike\n   444→                func(\n   445→                    result,\n   446→                    counts,\n   447→                    values,\n   448→                    comp_ids,\n   449→                    min_count=min_count,\n   450→                    mask=mask,\n   451→                    result_mask=result_mask,\n   452→                    **kwargs,\n   453→                )\n   454→            elif self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n   455→                func(\n   456→                    out=result,\n   457→                    values=values,\n   458→                    labels=comp_ids,\n   459→                    mask=mask,\n   460→                    result_mask=result_mask,\n   461→                    **kwargs,\n   462→                )\n   463→                result = result.astype(bool, copy=False)\n   464→            elif self.how in [\"skew\", \"kurt\"]:\n   465→                func(\n   466→                    out=result,\n   467→                    counts=counts,\n   468→                    values=values,\n   469→                    labels=comp_ids,\n   470→                    mask=mask,\n   471→                    result_mask=result_mask,\n   472→                    **kwargs,\n   473→                )\n   474→                if dtype == object:\n   475→                    result = result.astype(object)\n   476→\n   477→            else:\n   478→                raise NotImplementedError(f\"{self.how} is not implemented\")\n   479→        else:\n   480→            # TODO: min_count\n   481→            if self.how != \"rank\":\n   482→                # TODO: should rank take result_mask?\n   483→                kwargs[\"result_mask\"] = result_mask\n   484→            func(\n   485→                out=result,\n   486→                values=values,\n   487→                labels=comp_ids,\n   488→                ngroups=ngroups,\n   489→                is_datetimelike=is_datetimelike,\n   490→                mask=mask,\n   491→                **kwargs,\n   492→            )\n   493→\n   494→        if self.kind == \"aggregate\" and self.how not in [\"idxmin\", \"idxmax\"]:\n   495→            # i.e. counts is defined.  Locations where count<min_count\n   496→            # need to have the result set to np.nan, which may require casting,\n   497→            # see GH#40767. For idxmin/idxmax is handled specially via post-processing\n   498→            if result.dtype.kind in \"iu\" and not is_datetimelike:\n   499→                # if the op keeps the int dtypes, we have to use 0\n   500→                cutoff = max(0 if self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\"] else 1, min_count)\n   501→                empty_groups = counts < cutoff\n   502→                if empty_groups.any():\n   503→                    if result_mask is not None:\n   504→                        assert result_mask[empty_groups].all()\n   505→                    else:\n   506→                        # Note: this conversion could be lossy, see GH#40767\n   507→                        result = result.astype(\"float64\")\n   508→                        result[empty_groups] = np.nan\n   509→\n   510→        result = result.T\n   511→\n   512→        if self.how not in self.cast_blocklist:\n   513→            # e.g. if we are int64 and need to restore to datetime64/timedelta64\n   514→            # \"rank\" is the only member of cast_blocklist we get here\n   515→            # Casting only needed for float16, bool, datetimelike,\n   516→            #  and self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\", \"ohlc\", \"cumprod\"]\n   517→            res_dtype = self._get_result_dtype(orig_values.dtype)\n   518→            op_result = maybe_downcast_to_dtype(result, res_dtype)\n   519→        else:\n   520→            op_result = result\n   521→\n   522→        return op_result\n   523→\n   524→    @final\n   525→    def _validate_axis(self, axis: AxisInt, values: ArrayLike) -> None:\n   526→        if values.ndim > 2:\n   527→            raise NotImplementedError(\"number of dimensions is currently limited to 2\")\n   528→        if values.ndim == 2:\n   529→            assert axis  649→    @cache_readonly\n   650→    def indices(self) -> dict[Hashable, npt.NDArray[np.intp]]:\n   651→        \"\"\"dict {group name -> group indices}\"\"\"\n   652→        if len(self.groupings) == @final\n   675→    @cache_readonly\n   676→    def result_ilocs(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.intp]:\n   677→        \"\"\"\n   678→        Get the original integer locations of result_index in the input.\n   679→        \"\"\"\n   680→        # Original indices are where group_index would go via sorting.\n   681→        # But when dropna is true, we need to remove null values while accounting for\n   682→        # any gaps that then occur because of them.\n   683→        ids = self.ids\n   684→\n   685→        if self.has_dropped_na:\n   686→            mask = np.where(ids >= 0)\n   687→            # Count how many gaps are caused by previous null values for each position\n   688→            null_gaps = np.cumsum(ids == -1)[mask]\n   689→            ids = ids[mask]\n   690→\n   691→        result = get_group_index_sorter(ids, self.ngroups)\n   692→\n   693→        if self.has_dropped_na:\n   694→            # Shift by the number of prior null gaps\n   695→            result += np.take(null_gaps, result)\n   696→\n   697→        return result\n   698→\n   699→    @property\n   700→    def codes(self) -> list[npt.NDArray[np.signedinteger]]:\n   701→        return [ping.codes @property\n   712→    def names(self) -> list[Hashable]:\n   713→        return [ping.name for ping in self.groupings]\n   714→\n   715→    @final\n   716→    def size(self) -> Series:\n   717→        \"\"\"\n   718→        Compute group sizes.\n   719→        \"\"\"\n   720→        ids = self.ids\n   721→        ngroups = self.ngroups\n   722→        out: np.ndarray | list\n   723→        if np.ndarray[ndim=2]\n        *,\n        min_count: int,\n        ngroups: int,\n        comp_ids: np.ndarray,\n        mask: npt.NDArray[np.bool_] | None,\n        result_mask: npt.NDArray[np.bool_] | None,\n        initial: Any = 0,\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> np.ndarray:  # np.ndarray[ndim=2]\n        orig_values = values\n\n        dtype = values.dtype\n        is_numeric = dtype.kind in \"iufcb\"\n\n        is_datetimelike = dtype.kind in \"mM\"\n\n        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n            if mask is None:\n                mask = isna(values)\n\n        if is_datetimelike:\n            values = values.view(\"int64\")\n            is_numeric = True\n        elif dtype.kind == \"b\":\n            values = values.view(\"uint8\")\n        if values.dtype == \"float16\":\n            values = values.astype(np.float32)\n\n        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n            if dtype == object:\n                if kwargs[\"skipna\"]:\n                    # GH#37501: don't raise on pd.NA when skipna=True\n                    if mask is not None and mask.any():\n                        # mask on original values computed separately\n                        values = values.copy()\n                        values[mask] = True\n            values = values.astype(bool, copy=False).view(np.int8)\n            is_numeric = True\n\n        values = values.T\n        if mask is not None:\n            mask = mask.T\n            if result_mask is not None:\n                result_mask = result_mask.T\n\n        out_shape = self._get_output_shape(ngroups, values)\n        func = self._get_cython_function(self.kind, self.how, values.dtype, is_numeric)\n        values = self._get_cython_vals(values)\n        out_dtype = self._get_out_dtype(values.dtype)\n\n        result = maybe_fill(np.empty(out_shape, dtype=out_dtype))\n        if self.kind == \"aggregate\":\n            counts = np.zeros(ngroups, dtype=np.int64)\n            if self.how in [\n                \"idxmin\",\n                \"idxmax\",\n                \"min\",\n                \"max\",\n                \"mean\",\n                \"last\",\n                \"first\",\n                \"sum\",\n                \"median\",\n            ]:\n                if self.how == \"sum\":\n                    # TODO: min_count\n            if self.how != \"rank\":\n                # TODO: should rank take result_mask?\n                kwargs[\"result_mask\"] = result_mask\n            func(\n                out=result,\n                values=values,\n                labels=comp_ids,\n                ngroups=ngroups,\n                is_datetimelike=is_datetimelike,\n                mask=mask,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n\n        if self.kind == \"aggregate\" and self.how not in [\"idxmin\", \"idxmax\"]:\n            # i.e. counts is defined.  Locations where count<min_count\n            # need to have the result set to np.nan, which may require casting,\n            # see GH#40767. For idxmin/idxmax is handled specially via post-processing\n            if result.dtype.kind in \"iu\" and not is_datetimelike:\n                # if the op keeps the int dtypes, we have to use 0\n                cutoff = max(0 if self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\"] else 1, min_count)\n                empty_groups = counts < cutoff\n                if empty_groups.any():\n                    if result_mask is not None:\n                        assert result_mask[empty_groups].all()\n                    else:\n                        # Note: this conversion could be lossy, see GH#40767\n                        result = result.astype(\"float64\")\n                        result[empty_groups] = np.nan\n\n        result = result.T\n\n        if self.how not in self.cast_blocklist:\n            # e.g. if we are int64 and need to restore to datetime64/timedelta64\n            # \"rank\" is the only member of cast_blocklist we get here\n            # Casting only needed for float16, bool, datetimelike,\n            #  and self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\", \"ohlc\", \"cumprod\"]\n            res_dtype = self._get_result_dtype(orig_values.dtype)\n            op_result = maybe_downcast_to_dtype(result, res_dtype)\n        else:\n            op_result = result\n\n        return op_result\n\n    @final\n    def _validate_axis(self, axis: AxisInt, values: ArrayLike) -> @final\n    def _get_splitter(self, data: NDFrame) -> DataSplitter:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns\n        -------\n        Generator yielding subsetted objects\n        \"\"\"\n        if @cache_readonly\n    def result_ilocs(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.intp]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get the original integer locations of result_index in the input.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Original ids = self.ids\n\n        if self.has_dropped_na:\n            mask = np.where(ids >= 0)\n            # Count how many gaps are caused by previous null values for each position\n            null_gaps result_index.name = self.names[0]\n            ids = ensure_platform_int(self.codes[0])\n        elif all(obs):\n            result_index, ids = self._ob_index_and_ids(\n                levels, self.codes, self.names, sorts\n            )\n        elif not any(obs):\n            result_index, ids = self._unob_index_and_ids(levels, self.codes, self.names)\n        else:\n            # Combine unobserved and observed parts\n            names = self.names\n            codes = [ping.codes for ping in self.groupings]\n            ob_indices = [idx for idx, ob in enumerate(obs) if ob]\n            unob_indices = [idx for idx, ob in enumerate(obs) if not ob]\n            ob_index, ob_ids = self._ob_index_and_ids(\n                levels=[levels[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n drop_levels = [\n                    n_levels - idx\n                    for idx, sort in enumerate(reversed(sorts), 1)\n                    if not sort\n                ]\n                if verify_integrity=False,\n        )\n        if not consistent_sorting and len(ob_index) > 0:\n            # Sort by the levels where the corresponding sort argument is True\n            n_levels =       return ob_index, ob_ids\n\n    def _unob_index_and_ids(\n        self,\n        levels: list[Index],\n        codes: list[npt.NDArray[np.intp]],\n        names: list[Hashable],\n    ) -> ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Aggregation functions\n\n    @final\n    def _cython_operation(\n        self,\n        kind: str,\n        values,\n        how: str,\n        axis: AxisInt,\n        min_count: int = -1,\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> ArrayLike:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns the values of a cython operation.\n        \"\"\"\n        assert kind in [\"transform\", \"aggregate\"]\n\n        cy_op = WrappedCythonOp(kind=kind, how=how, has_dropped_na=self.has_dropped_na)\n\n        return cy_op.cython_operation(\n            values=values,\n            axis=axis,\n            min_count=min_count,\n            comp_ids=self.ids,\n            ngroups=self.ngroups,\n            **kwargs,\n        )\n\n    @final\n    def agg_series(\n        self, obj: Series, func: Callable, preserve_dtype: bool = False\n    ) -> ArrayLike:\n        \"\"\"\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        obj : Series\n        func : function taking a Series and returning a scalar-like\n        preserve_dtype : bool\n            Whether the aggregation is known to be dtype-preserving.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        np.ndarray or ExtensionArray\n        \"\"\"\n        # Try the fast path first for numeric numpy arrays.\n        # This uses C-level numpy reduceat operations and avoids Python object\n        # creation overhead per group.\n        values = obj._values\n        if isinstance(values, np.ndarray) and values.dtype.kind in \"iufcb\":\n            try:\n                result = self._aggregate_series_fast(obj, func)\n                return obj.array._cast_pointwise_result(result)\n            except (TypeError, ValueError, NotImplementedError):\n                pass\n\n        result = self._aggregate_series_pure_python(obj, func)\n        return obj.array._cast_pointwise_result(result)\n\n    @final\n    def _aggregate_series_fast(\n        self, obj: Series, func: Callable\n    ) -> npt.NDArray[np.object_]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fast series aggregation using Cython-optimized hash table-based grouping\n        and vectorized numpy reduceat operations.\n\n        For common numeric aggregation functions (sum, mean, min, max, prod),\n        uses numpy ufunc.reduceat which operates at C level over pre-sorted,\n        cache-friendly contiguous memory, avoiding Python object creation per group.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        obj : Series\n            The series to aggregate.\n        func : Callable\n            The aggregation function.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        np.ndarray[object]\n        \"\"\"\n        ngroups = self.ngroups\n        if ngroups == 0:\n            return np.empty(0, dtype=\"O\")\n\n        # Use cached pre-sorted structure (result_ilocs and _sorted_ids are\n        # computed once and cached via @cache_readonly, leveraging pandas'\n        # internal khash-based group index computation).\n        sort_idx = self.result_ilocs\n        sorted_ids = self._sorted_ids\n\n        # Generate group boundary slices at C level\n        starts, ends = lib.generate_slices(sorted_ids, ngroups)\n\n        values = obj._values\n        # Only handle numpy numeric arrays in fast path\n        if not (isinstance(values, np.ndarray) and values.dtype.kind in \"iufcb\"):\n            raise NotImplementedError(\"Fast path only supports numeric numpy arrays\")\n\n        func_name = getattr(func, \"__name__\", \"\") or \"\"\n\n        # Fast path: use numpy ufunc.reduceat for common aggregation functions.\n        # reduceat performs C-level reduction over contiguous array segments,\n        # which is orders of magnitude faster than iterating over Python Series.\n        if func_name in _UFUNC_REDUCEAT_MAP:\n            ufunc = _UFUNC_REDUCEAT_MAP[func_name]\n            sorted_values = values[sort_idx].astype(np.float64)\n\n            non_empty = starts < ends\n            if non_empty.all():\n                # Simple common case: all groups have data\n                result = ufunc.reduceat(sorted_values, starts)\n            else:\n                # Some groups are empty - filter starts, compute, then map back\n                ne_starts = starts[non_empty]\n                empty_val = _UFUNC_EMPTY_VALUE.get(func_name, np.nan)\n                result = np.full(ngroups, empty_val, dtype=np.float64)\n                if len(ne_starts) > 0:\n                    result[non_empty] = ufunc.reduceat(sorted_values, ne_starts)\n\n            return result.astype(object)\n\n        # Fast path for mean: sum / count using reduceat\n        if func_name in _MEAN_FUNC_NAMES:\n            sorted_values = values[sort_idx].astype(np.float64)\n            non_empty = starts < ends\n            result = np.full(ngroups, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n            if non_empty.any():\n                ne_starts = starts[non_empty]\n                partial_sums = np.add.reduceat(sorted_values, ne_starts)\n                counts = (ends - starts).astype(np.float64)\n                result[non_empty] = partial_sums / counts[non_empty]\n            return result.astype(object)\n\n        # General numeric case: optimized Python loop using pre-sorted data.\n        # Pre-sorting allows cache-friendly sequential memory access and avoids\n        # re-sorting on each iteration.\n        sorted_obj = obj.take(sort_idx)\n        result_arr = np.empty(ngroups, dtype=\"O\")\n        initialized = False\n        dtype = obj.dtype\n\n        for i in range(ngroups):\n            start_i = int(starts[i])\n            end_i = int(ends[i])\n            mgr = sorted_obj._mgr.get_slice(slice(start_i, end_i))\n            group = sorted_obj._constructor_from_mgr(mgr, axes=mgr.axes)\n            group._name = obj.name\n\n            res = func(group)\n            res = extract_result(res)\n\n np.ndarray[ndim=2]\n        orig_values = values\n\n        dtype = values.dtype\n        is_numeric = dtype.kind in \"iufcb\"\n\n        is_datetimelike = dtype.kind in \"mM\"\n\n        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n            if mask is None:\n                mask = isna(values)\n\n        if is_datetimelike:\n            values = values.view(\"int64\")\n            is_numeric = True\n        elif dtype.kind == \"b\":\n            values = values.view(\"uint8\")\n        if values.dtype == \"float16\":\n            values = values.astype(np.float32)\n\n        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n            if dtype == object:\n                if kwargs[\"skipna\"]:\n                    # GH#37501: don't raise on pd.NA when skipna=True\n                    if mask is not None and mask.any():\n                        # mask on original values computed separately\n                        values = values.copy()\n                        values[mask] = True\n            values = values.astype(bool, copy=False).view(np.int8)\n            is_numeric = True\n\n        values = values.T\n        if mask is not None:\n            mask = mask.T\n            if result_mask is not None:\n                result_mask = result_mask.T\n\n        out_shape = self._get_output_shape(ngroups, values)\n        func = self._get_cython_function(self.kind, self.how, values.dtype, is_numeric)\n        values = self._get_cython_vals(values)\n        out_dtype = self._get_out_dtype(values.dtype)\n\n        result = maybe_fill(np.empty(out_shape, dtype=out_dtype))\n        if self.kind == \"aggregate\":\n            counts = np.zeros(ngroups, dtype=np.int64)\n            if self.how in [\n                \"idxmin\",\n                \"idxmax\",\n                \"min\",\n                \"max\",\n                \"mean\",\n                \"last\",\n                \"first\",\n                \"sum\",\n                \"median\",\n            ]:\n                if self.how == \"sum\":\n                    # pass in through kwargs only for sum (other functions don't have\n                    # the keyword)\n                    kwargs[\"initial\"] = initial\n                func(\n                    out=result,\n                    counts=counts,\n                    values=values,\n                    labels=comp_ids,\n                    min_count=min_count,\n                    mask=mask,\n                    result_mask=result_mask,\n                    is_datetimelike=is_datetimelike,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n            elif self.how in [\"sem\", \"std\", \"var\", \"ohlc\", \"prod\"]:\n                if self.how in [\"std\", \"sem\"]:\n                    kwargs[\"is_datetimelike\"] = is_datetimelike\n                func(\n                    result,\n                    counts,\n                    values,\n                    comp_ids,\n                    min_count=min_count,\n                    mask=mask,\n                    result_mask=result_mask,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n            elif self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n                func(\n                    out=result,\n                    values=values,\n                    labels=comp_ids,\n                    mask=mask,\n                    result_mask=result_mask,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n                result = result.astype(bool, copy=False)\n            elif self.how in [\"skew\", \"kurt\"]:\n                func(\n                    out=result,\n                    counts=counts,\n                    values=values,\n                    labels=comp_ids,\n                    mask=mask,\n                    result_mask=result_mask,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n                if dtype == object:\n                    result = result.astype(object)\n\n            else:\n                raise NotImplementedError(f\"{self.how} is not implemented\")\n        else:\n            # TODO: min_count\n            if self.how != \"rank\":\n                # TODO: should rank take result_mask?\n                kwargs[\"result_mask\"] = result_mask\n            func(\n                out=result,\n                values=values,\n                labels=comp_ids,\n                ngroups=ngroups,\n                is_datetimelike=is_datetimelike,\n                mask=mask,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n\n        if self.kind == \"aggregate\" and self.how not in [\"idxmin\", \"idxmax\"]:\n            # i.e. counts is defined.  Locations where count<min_count\n            # need to have the result set to np.nan, which may require casting,\n            # see GH#40767. For idxmin/idxmax is handled specially via post-processing\n            if result.dtype.kind in \"iu\" and not is_datetimelike:\n                # if the op keeps the int dtypes, we have to use 0\n                cutoff = max(0 if self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\"] else 1, min_count)\n                empty_groups = counts < cutoff\n                if empty_groups.any():\n                    if result_mask is not None:\n                        assert result_mask[empty_groups].all()\n                    else:\n                        # Note: this conversion could be lossy, see GH#40767\n                        result = result.astype(\"float64\")\n                        result[empty_groups] = np.nan\n\n        result = result.T\n\n        if self.how not in self.cast_blocklist:\n            # e.g. if we are int64 and need to restore to datetime64/timedelta64\n            # \"rank\" is the only member of cast_blocklist we get here\n            # Casting only needed for float16, bool, datetimelike,\n            #  and self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\", \"ohlc\", \"cumprod\"]\n            res_dtype = self._get_result_dtype(orig_values.dtype)\n            op_result = maybe_downcast_to_dtype(result, res_dtype)\n        else:\n            op_result = result\n\n        return op_result\n\n    @final\n    def _validate_axis(self, axis: AxisInt, values: ArrayLike) -> None:\n        if values.ndim > 2:\n            raise NotImplementedError(\"number of dimensions is currently limited to 2\")\n        if values.ndim == 2:\n            assert axis == 1, axis\n        elif not is_1d_only_ea_dtype(values.dtype):\n            # Note: it is *not* the case that axis is always 0 for 1-dim values,\n            #  as we can have 1D ExtensionArrays that we need to treat as 2D\n            assert axis == 0\n\n    @final\n    def cython_operation(\n        self,\n        *,\n        values: ArrayLike,\n        axis: AxisInt,\n        min_count: int = -1,\n        comp_ids: np.ndarray,\n        ngroups: int,\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> ArrayLike:\n        \"\"\"\n        Call our cython function, with appropriate pre- and post- processing.\n        \"\"\"\n **kwargs,\n        )\n\n\nclass BaseGrouper:\n    \"\"\"\n    This is an internal Grouper class, which actually holds\n    the generated groups\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    axis : Index\n @final\n    def _get_splitter(self, data: NDFrame) -> DataSplitter:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns\n        -------\n        Generator yielding subsetted objects\n        \"\"\"\n        if npt.NDArray[np.intp]]:\n        \"\"\"dict {group name -> group indices}\"\"\"\n        if len(self.groupings) == 1 and isinstance(self.result_index, CategoricalIndex):\n            # This shows @cache_readonly\n    def result_ilocs(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.intp]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get the original integer locations of result_index in the input.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Original indices are where group_index would go via sorting.\n        # But when dropna is true, we need to remove null values while accounting for\n        # any gaps that then occur because of them.\n        ids = self.ids\n\n        if self.has_dropped_na:\n            mask = np.where(ids >= 0)\n            # Count how many gaps are caused by previous null values for each position\n            null_gaps copy=False) for ping in self.groupings\n        ]\n        obs = [\n            ping._observed or not ping._passed_categorical for ping in self.groupings\n        ]\n        sorts = [ping._sort for ping in self.groupings]\n        # When passed a categorical grouping, keep all categories\n        for k, (ping, level) in enumerate(zip(self.groupings, levels, strict=True)):\n            if ping._passed_categorical:\n                levels[k] = level.set_categories(ping._orig_cats)\n\n        if len(self.groupings) == 1:\n            result_index = levels[0]\n            result_index.name = self.names[0]\n            ids = ensure_platform_int(self.codes[0])\n        elif all(obs):\n            result_index, ids = self._ob_index_and_ids(\n                levels, self.codes, self.names, sorts\n            )\n        elif not any(obs):\n            result_index, ids = self._unob_index_and_ids(levels, self.codes, self.names)\n        else:\n            # Combine unobserved and observed parts\n            names = self.names\n            codes = [ping.codes for ping in self.groupings]\n            ob_indices = [idx for idx, ob in enumerate(obs) if ob]\n            unob_indices = [idx for idx, ob in enumerate(obs) if not ob]\n            ob_index, ob_ids = self._ob_index_and_ids(\n                levels=[levels[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n                codes=[codes[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n                names=[names[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n                sorts=[sorts[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n            )\n            unob_index, np.repeat(ob_index.codes, len(unob_index), axis=1),\n                ],\n                axis=0,\n            )\n            _, index = np.unique(unob_indices + ob_indices, return_index=True)\n            result_index = MultiIndex(\n                levels=list(unob_index.levels) + list(ob_index.levels),\n                codes=result_index_codes,\n                names=list(unob_index.names) + result_index = result_index.take(sorter)\n                _, index = np.unique(sorter, return_index=True)\n                ids = ensure_platform_int(ids)\n                ids = index.take(ids)\n verify_integrity=False,\n        )\n        if not consistent_sorting and len(ob_index) > 0:\n            # Sort by the levels where the corresponding sort argument is True\n            n_levels = len(drop_levels) > 0:\n                sorter = ob_index._drop_level_numbers(drop_levels).argsort()\n            else:\n                sorter = ob_index.argsort()\n            ob_index =       return ob_index, ob_ids\n\n    def _unob_index_and_ids(\n        self,\n        levels: list[Index],\n        codes: list[npt.NDArray[np.intp]],\n        names: list[Hashable],\n    ) ->        # Note: only called from _insert_inaxis_grouper, which\n        #  is only called for BaseGrouper, never for BinGrouper\n        result_index = self.result_index\n        if len(self.groupings) ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Aggregation functions\n\n    @final\n    def _cython_operation(\n        self,\n        kind: str,\n        values,\n        how: str,\n        axis: AxisInt,\n        min_count: int = -1,\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> ArrayLike:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns the values of a cython operation.\n        \"\"\"\n        assert kind in [\"transform\", \"aggregate\"]\n\n        cy_op = WrappedCythonOp(kind=kind, how=how, has_dropped_na=self.has_dropped_na)\n\n        return cy_op.cython_operation(\n            values=values,\n            axis=axis,\n            min_count=min_count,\n            comp_ids=self.ids,\n            ngroups=self.ngroups,\n            **kwargs,\n        )\n\n    @final\n    def agg_series(\n        self, obj: Series, func: Callable, preserve_dtype: bool = False\n    ) -> ArrayLike:\n        \"\"\"\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        obj : Series\n        func : function taking a Series and returning a scalar-like\n        preserve_dtype : bool\n            Whether the aggregation is known to be dtype-preserving.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        np.ndarray or ExtensionArray\n        \"\"\"\n        # Try the fast path first for numeric numpy arrays.\n        # This uses C-level numpy reduceat operations and avoids Python\n        # object creation overhead per group.\n        values = obj._values\n        if isinstance(values, np.ndarray) and values.dtype.kind in \"iufcb\":\n            try:\n                result = self._aggregate_series_fast(obj, func)\n                return obj.array._cast_pointwise_result(result)\n            except (TypeError, ValueError, NotImplementedError):\n                pass\n\n        result = self._aggregate_series_pure_python(obj, func)\n        return obj.array._cast_pointwise_result(result)\n\n    @final\n    def _aggregate_series_fast(\n        self, obj: Series, func: Callable\n    ) -> npt.NDArray[np.object_]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fast series aggregation using vectorized numpy reduceat operations.\n\n        Leverages pandas' internal khash-based group index (result_ilocs,\n        _sorted_ids) to pre-sort data once, then uses numpy ufunc.reduceat\n        for C-level group reduction without Python object creation per group.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        obj : Series\n        func : Callable\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        np.ndarray[object]\n        \"\"\"\n        ngroups = self.ngroups\n        if ngroups == 0:\n            return np.empty(0, dtype=\"O\")\n\n        # Use cached pre-sorted group structure based on pandas' internal\n        # khash hash table group index computation.\n        sort_idx = self.result_ilocs\n        sorted_ids = self._sorted_ids\n\n        # Generate group boundary slices at C level using khash-sorted indices\n        starts, ends = lib.generate_slices(sorted_ids, ngroups)\n\n        values = obj._values\n        if not (isinstance(values, np.ndarray) and values.dtype.kind in \"iufcb\"):\n            raise NotImplementedError(\"Fast path requires numeric numpy array\")\n\n        func_name = getattr(func, \"__name__\", \"\") or \"\"\n\n        # Use numpy ufunc.reduceat for common aggregation functions.\n        # reduceat performs C-level reduction over contiguous memory segments,\n        # avoiding Python Series object creation per group.\n        if func_name in _UFUNC_REDUCEAT_MAP:\n            ufunc = _UFUNC_REDUCEAT_MAP[func_name]\n            sorted_values = values[sort_idx].astype(np.float64)\n\n            non_empty = starts < ends\n            if non_empty.all():\n                # Fast common case: all groups have data\n                result = ufunc.reduceat(sorted_values, starts)\n            else:\n                # Handle empty groups: filter, compute, then map back\n                ne_starts = starts[non_empty]\n                empty_val = _UFUNC_EMPTY_VALUE.get(func_name, np.nan)\n                result = np.full(ngroups, empty_val, dtype=np.float64)\n                if len(ne_starts) > 0:\n                    result[non_empty] = ufunc.reduceat(sorted_values, ne_starts)\n\n            return result.astype(object)\n\n        # Mean: use sum/count via reduceat\n        if func_name in _MEAN_FUNC_NAMES:\n            sorted_values = values[sort_idx].astype(np.float64)\n            non_empty = starts < ends\n            result = np.full(ngroups, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n            if non_empty.any():\n                ne_starts = starts[non_empty]\n                partial_sums = np.add.reduceat(sorted_values, ne_starts)\n                counts = (ends - starts).astype(np.float64)\n                result[non_empty] = partial_sums / counts[non_empty]\n            return result.astype(object)\n\n        # General numeric case: optimized loop using pre-sorted contiguous data.\n        # Pre-sorting data once allows cache-efficient sequential access.\n        sorted_obj = obj.take(sort_idx)\n        result_arr = np.empty(ngroups, dtype=\"O\")\n        initialized = False\n        dtype = obj.dtype\n\n        for i in range(ngroups):\n            start_i = int(starts[i])\n            end_i = int(ends[i])\n            mgr = sorted_obj._mgr.get_slice(slice(start_i, end_i))\n np.ndarray[ndim=2]\n        orig_values = values\n\n        dtype = values.dtype\n        is_numeric = dtype.kind in \"iufcb\"\n\n        is_datetimelike = dtype.kind in \"mM\"\n\n        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n            if mask is None:\n                mask = isna(values)\n\n        if is_datetimelike:\n            values = values.view(\"int64\")\n            is_numeric = True\n        elif dtype.kind == \"b\":\n            values = values.view(\"uint8\")\n        if values.dtype == \"float16\":\n            values = values.astype(np.float32)\n\n        if self.how in [\"any\", \"all\"]:\n            if dtype == object:\n                if kwargs[\"skipna\"]:\n                    # TODO: min_count\n            if self.how != \"rank\":\n                # TODO: should rank take result_mask?\n                kwargs[\"result_mask\"] = result_mask\n            func(\n                out=result,\n                values=values,\n                labels=comp_ids,\n                ngroups=ngroups,\n                is_datetimelike=is_datetimelike,\n                mask=mask,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n\n        if self.kind == \"aggregate\" and self.how not in [\"idxmin\", \"idxmax\"]:\n            # i.e. counts is defined.  Locations where count<min_count\n            # need to have the result set to np.nan, which may require casting,\n            # see GH#40767. For idxmin/idxmax is handled specially via post-processing\n            if result.dtype.kind in \"iu\" and not is_datetimelike:\n                # if the op keeps the int dtypes, we have to use 0\n                cutoff = max(0 if self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\"] else 1, min_count)\n                empty_groups = counts < cutoff\n                if empty_groups.any():\n                    if result_mask is not None:\n                        assert result_mask[empty_groups].all()\n                    else:\n                        # Note: this conversion could be lossy, see GH#40767\n                        result = result.astype(\"float64\")\n                        result[empty_groups] = np.nan\n\n        result = result.T\n\n        if self.how not in self.cast_blocklist:\n            # e.g. if we are int64 and need to restore to datetime64/timedelta64\n            # \"rank\" is the only member of cast_blocklist we get here\n            # Casting only needed for float16, bool, datetimelike,\n            #  and self.how in [\"sum\", \"prod\", \"ohlc\", \"cumprod\"]\n            res_dtype = self._get_result_dtype(orig_values.dtype)\n            op_result = maybe_downcast_to_dtype(result, res_dtype)\n        else:\n            op_result = result\n\n        return op_result\n\n    @final\n    def _validate_axis(self, axis: AxisInt, values: ArrayLike) -> @final\n    def _get_splitter(self, data: NDFrame) -> DataSplitter:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns\n        -------\n        Generator yielding subsetted objects\n        \"\"\"\n        if npt.NDArray[np.intp]]:\n        \"\"\"dict {group name -> group indices}\"\"\"\n        if len(self.groupings) == 1 and isinstance(self.result_index, CategoricalIndex):\n            # This shows @cache_readonly\n    def result_ilocs(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.intp]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get the original integer locations of result_index in the input.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Original indices are where group_index would go via sorting.\n        # But when dropna is true, we need to remove null values while accounting for\n        # any gaps that then occur because of them.\n        ids = self.ids\n\n        if self.has_dropped_na:\n            mask = np.where(ids >= 0)\n            # Count how many gaps are caused by previous null values for each position\n            null_gaps = np.cumsum(ids == -1)[mask]\n            ids = ids[mask]\n\n        result = get_group_index_sorter(ids, self.ngroups)\n\n        if self.has_dropped_na:\n            # Shift by the number of prior copy=False) for ping in self.groupings\n        ]\n        obs = [\n            ping._observed or not ping._passed_categorical for ping in self.groupings\n        ]\n        sorts = [ping._sort for ping in self.groupings]\n        # When passed a categorical grouping, keep all categories\n        for k, (ping, level) in enumerate(zip(self.groupings, levels, strict=True)):\n            if ping._passed_categorical:\n                levels[k] = level.set_categories(ping._orig_cats)\n\n        if len(self.groupings) == 1:\n            result_index = levels[0]\n            result_index.name = self.names[0]\n            ids = ensure_platform_int(self.codes[0])\n        elif all(obs):\n            result_index, ids = self._ob_index_and_ids(\n                levels, self.codes, self.names, sorts\n            )\n        elif not any(obs):\n            result_index, ids = self._unob_index_and_ids(levels, self.codes, self.names)\n        else:\n            # Combine unobserved and observed parts\n            names = self.names\n            codes = [ping.codes for ping in self.groupings]\n            ob_indices = [idx for idx, ob in enumerate(obs) if ob]\n            unob_indices = [idx for idx, ob in enumerate(obs) if not ob]\n            ob_index, ob_ids = self._ob_index_and_ids(\n                levels=[levels[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n                codes=[codes[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n                names=[names[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n                sorts=[sorts[idx] for idx in ob_indices],\n            )\n            unob_index, np.repeat(ob_index.codes, len(unob_index), axis=1),\n                ],\n                axis=0,\n            )\n            _, index = np.unique(unob_indices + ob_indices, return_index=True)\n            result_index = MultiIndex(\n                levels=list(unob_index.levels) + list(ob_index.levels),\n                codes=result_index_codes,\n                names=list(unob_index.names) + drop_levels = [\n                    n_levels - idx\n                    for idx, sort in enumerate(reversed(sorts), 1)\n                    if not sort\n                ]\n                if result_index = result_index.take(sorter)\n                _, index = np.unique(sorter, return_index=True)\n                ids = ensure_platform_int(ids)\n                ids = index.take(ids)\n verify_integrity=False,\n        )\n        if not consistent_sorting and len(ob_index) > 0:\n            # Sort by the levels where the corresponding sort argument is True\n            n_levels =       return ob_index, ob_ids\n\n    def _unob_index_and_ids(\n        self,\n        levels: list[Index],\n        codes: list[npt.NDArray[np.intp]],\n        names: list[Hashable],\n    ) -> ------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Aggregation functions\n\n    @final\n    def _cython_operation(\n        self,\n        kind: str,\n        values,\n        how: str,\n        axis: AxisInt,\n        min_count: int = -1,\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> ArrayLike:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns the values of a cython operation.\n        \"\"\"\n        assert kind in [\"transform\", \"aggregate\"]\n\n        cy_op = WrappedCythonOp(kind=kind, how=how, has_dropped_na=self.has_dropped_na)\n\n        return cy_op.cython_operation(\n            values=values,\n            axis=axis,\n            min_count=min_count,\n            comp_ids=self.ids,\n            ngroups=self.ngroups,\n            **kwargs,\n        )\n\n    @final\n    def agg_series(\n        self, obj: Series, func: Callable, preserve_dtype: bool = False\n    ) -> ArrayLike:\n        \"\"\"\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        obj : Series\n        func : function taking a Series and returning a scalar-like\n        preserve_dtype : bool\n            Whether the aggregation is known to be dtype-preserving.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        np.ndarray or ExtensionArray\n        \"\"\"\n        # Try the fast path first for numeric numpy arrays.\n        # This uses C-level numpy reduceat operations and avoids Python\n        # object creation overhead per group.\n        values = obj._values\n        if isinstance(values, np.ndarray) and values.dtype.kind in \"iufcb\":\n            try:\n                result = self._aggregate_series_fast(obj, func)\n                return obj.array._cast_pointwise_result(result)\n            except (TypeError, ValueError, NotImplementedError):\n                pass\n\n        result = self._aggregate_series_pure_python(obj, func)\n        return obj.array._cast_pointwise_result(result)\n\n    @final\n    def _aggregate_series_fast(\n        self, obj: Series, func: Callable\n    ) -> npt.NDArray[np.object_]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Fast series aggregation using vectorized numpy reduceat operations.\n\n        Leverages pandas' internal khash-based group index (result_ilocs,\n        _sorted_ids) to pre-sort data once, then uses numpy ufunc.reduceat\n        for C-level group reduction without Python object creation per group.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        obj : Series\n        func : Callable\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        np.ndarray[object]\n        \"\"\"\n        ngroups = self.ngroups\n        if ngroups == 0:\n            return np.empty(0, dtype=\"O\")\n\n        # Use cached pre-sorted group structure based on pandas' internal\n        # khash hash table group index computation.\n        sort_idx = self.result_ilocs\n        sorted_ids = self._sorted_ids\n\n        # Generate group boundary slices at C level using khash-sorted indices\n        starts, ends = lib.generate_slices(sorted_ids, ngroups)\n\n        values = obj._values\n        if not (isinstance(values, np.ndarray) and values.dtype.kind in \"iufcb\"):\n            raise NotImplementedError(\"Fast path requires numeric numpy array\")\n\n        func_name = getattr(func, \"__name__\", \"\") or \"\"\n\n        # Use numpy ufunc.reduceat for common aggregation functions.\n        # reduceat performs C-level reduction over contiguous memory segments,\n        # avoiding Python Series object creation per group.\n        if func_name in _UFUNC_REDUCEAT_MAP:\n            ufunc = _UFUNC_REDUCEAT_MAP[func_name]\n            sorted_values = values[sort_idx].astype(np.float64)\n\n            non_empty = starts < ends\n            if non_empty.all():\n                # Fast common case: all groups have data\n                result = ufunc.reduceat(sorted_values, starts)\n            else:\n                # Handle empty groups: filter, compute, then map back\n                ne_starts = starts[non_empty]\n                empty_val = _UFUNC_EMPTY_VALUE.get(func_name, np.nan)\n                result = np.full(ngroups, empty_val, dtype=np.float64)\n                if len(ne_starts) > 0:\n                    result[non_empty] = ufunc.reduceat(sorted_values, ne_starts)\n\n            return result.astype(object)\n\n        # Mean: use sum/count via reduceat\n        if func_name in _MEAN_FUNC_NAMES:\n            sorted_values = values[sort_idx].astype(np.float64)\n            non_empty = starts < ends\n            result = np.full(ngroups, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)\n            if non_empty.any():\n                ne_starts = starts[non_empty]\n                partial_sums = np.add.reduceat(sorted_values, ne_starts)\n                counts = (ends - starts).astype(np.float64)\n                result[non_empty] = partial_sums / counts[non_empty]\n            return result.astype(object)\n\n        # General numeric case: optimized loop using pre-sorted contiguous data.\n        # Pre-sorting data once allows cache-efficient sequential access.\n        sorted_obj = obj.take(sort_idx)\n        result_arr = np.empty(ngroups, dtype=\"O\")\n        initialized = False\n        dtype = obj.dtype\n\n        for i in range(ngroups):\n            start_i = int(starts[i])\n            end_i = int(ends[i])\n            mgr = sorted_obj._mgr.get_slice(slice(start_i, end_i))\n            group = sorted_obj._constructor_from_mgr(mgr, axes=mgr.axes)\n            group._name = obj.name\n\n            res = func(group)\n            res = extract_result(res)\n\n            if not initialized:\n                check_result_array(res, dtype)\n                initialized = True\n\n            result_arr[i] = res\n\n        return result_arr\n\n    @final\n    def _aggregate_series_pure_python(\n        self, obj: Series, func: Callable\n    ) -> npt.NDArray[np.object_]:\n        result = np.empty(self.ngroups, dtype=\"O\")\n        initialized = False\n\n        splitter = self._get_splitter(obj)\n\n        for i, group in enumerate(splitter):\n            res = func(group)\n            res = extract_result(res)\n\n            if not initialized:\n                # We only do this validation on the first iteration\n                check_result_array(res, group.dtype)\n                initialized = True\n\n            result[i] = res\n\n        return
